 The same `stopChild` stops a frontend server: spawn `node_modules/.bin/vite` (not `npx vite`) and
 strip ANSI colors before matching its `Local:` line.
 
-Every route answers JSON; read the field for the route (`guide wire` has the rest):
-
-| Request | HTTP | Body | Read |
-|---|---|---|---|
-| `POST /q/<query>` answered | 200 | `{data, complete, cut, next, seq, tables, viewer}` | `body.data`: a list, a row, a shape, or a scalar (a keyed view read such as `totals[owner]` is just the number) |
-| `POST /q/<query>` refused | 200 | `{denied: {code, message, site?, rule?}}` | `body.denied.code` |
-| `POST /m/<mutation>` sent | 200 | `{state: "sent", id, seq, result}` | `body.result`: what the mutation returns (an inserted row, so its id is `body.result.id`); top-level `id` is the write id you sent, `null` without one |
-| `POST /m/<mutation>` refused | 200 | `{state: "failed", id, why: {code, message, site?, rule?}, current?}` | `body.why.code`: `E_RULE`, `NOT_FOUND`, `E_CONSTRAINT`, your `require`/`unique` code, `E_INPUT` for a value checked while running |
-| a request that does not decode (bad JSON type, unknown argument) | 400 | `{denied: {code: "E_INPUT"}}` | `body.denied.code` |
-| an undeclared persona, no credential | 401 | `{denied: {code: "E_AUTH"}}` | `body.denied.code` |
-| `POST /seed` | 200 | `{seeded: N}`, or `{denied}` with nothing written | `body.seeded` |
-| `POST /dev/reset` | 200 | `{reset: true, seeded: N, storeId, …}`, or `{denied}` with nothing reset | `body.reset` |
+Every route answers JSON; `guide wire --section envelopes` has the wire/client envelope table.
 
 A `unique ... else CODE` refuses with your code; without one, a clash is `E_CONSTRAINT` naming the index.
 `/seed` takes a JSON array, writes as the system and
@@ -257,3 +259,43 @@ skips rules, with fixture-row semantics (defaults, sequences); seed parents and
 It is an upsert by `id`: a reseed updates mutable columns, and changing an `immutable` one refuses
 `E_CONSTRAINT` (use a new id, `POST /dev/reset`, or restart with `--memory`). Personas, `/seed` and `/dev/reset` exist only under `dev`,
 and a persona must be declared in `use personas` (an outsider is 401 `E_AUTH`).
+
+## Browser proof: the exported app writes
+
+Run `npm run build:web`, then `snapback4 witness web dist --as alice --mutation add --args '{"title":"Browser proof"}'`.
+Password accounts: `--email proof@example.test --password-env PROOF_PASSWORD`
+(sign up first on an existing backend); `--url URL` selects that backend. Without it the
+witness owns a fresh `dev --memory` and uses the signup form for password accounts. Set `SNAPBACK4_CHROMIUM` if Chromium is
+not found. Starters register their SDK client; custom apps call
+`registerWebWitness(app, api)` from `snapback4/witness-web`, active only under
+`?witness=web`. This proves exported app/authenticated SDK integration; test
+its composer UI separately. Fresh browser/storage and a fresh default backend
+make runs repeatable; `--url` repeats use a stable write id, never reset.
+
+Never wait for `networkidle`: live pages hold a long poll. Navigate with
+`domcontentloaded`, wait for the control, and assert `sent` before cross-device
+reads. A predicted row or `pending` is not a server commit. Spawn servers
+directly via `resolveBinary()` or `node_modules/.bin/vite`, never via `npx`;
+stop only your children (recipe above). Rebuild the Expo web export when
+`EXPO_PUBLIC_SNAPBACK_URL` changes: starter `build:web` uses
+`expo export --platform web --clear`. Both starters accept runtime `?api=URL`.
+
+## Authoring and proof refusals
+
+N_FIXTURE_ARGUMENT_DRIFT means a fixture case no longer fits its operation signature. Update its arguments and run snapback4 test.
+
+E_WITNESS_BROWSER means no usable headless Chromium executable. Set SNAPBACK4_CHROMIUM.
+
+E_WITNESS_AUTH means the exported app did not sign in. Check the persona or credentials and register its client.
+
+E_WITNESS_MUTATION means no matching mutation was registered. Pass --mutation with a generated API name.
+
+E_WITNESS_ARGUMENT means an argument needs application values. Pass --args with valid values.
+
+E_WITNESS_EXPORT means the export has no index.html. Run build:web first.
+
+E_WITNESS_READY means the app could not open its write custody. Repair the reported schema or storage refusal.
+
+E_WITNESS_SENT means the write failed, stayed pending or timed out. Inspect the refusal; pending is not success.
+
+E_WITNESS_WEB means the browser proof could not complete. Check the export, browser and application.

+## Wire and client envelopes
+
+| Call | Success | Refusal / pending |
+|---|---|---|
+| `POST /m/<op>` | `{state:"sent", id?, seq, result, generation, store_id, head}`; inserted row: `result.id` | `{state:"failed", id?, why, current?, store_id, head}`; decode/auth/unknown-op failures can be `{denied}` |
+| `POST /q/<op>` | `{data, complete, cut, next, seq, tables, viewer, generation, store_id}`; list, row, shape or scalar | `{denied, complete:false, …}` or `{loading:true,data:null,complete:false,…}`; loading data is a placeholder, never an empty answer |
+| `POST /assets` raw bytes | `{asset:{id,type,bytes,width?,height?,duration?,name?}}`; `?as=file&name=…` for files | `{denied}`; pass `{id:asset.id}` to the mutation |
+| `await client.query(api.op, args)` | `Read<T>`: `{data, existence, complete, …}`; narrow with `isReady` | `{denied}`; one-shot reads settle or refuse; observers can also load |
+| `await client.mutate(api.op, args)` | `Write<T>`: `{state:"sent", id, seq, result?, …}` | `{state:"failed", id, why, …}` or `{state:"pending", id, …}`; pending is not success |
+
+SDK envelopes differ from raw HTTP. Check `loading` and `denied` before `data`; HTTP status alone is insufficient.
+An omitted mutation request id is omitted from its reply, never the inserted
+row id. Send an id once per intended write to retry safely; SDK clients mint
+it. Replays omit historical result/current; see the Operations contract below.
+

+For a browser proof, register the application's own app and generated API
+(starters do this; the hook is active only under `?witness=web`):
+
+```ts
+import { registerWebWitness } from "snapback4/witness-web";
+import { createApp } from "snapback4/app";
+import { localStorageSessionStore } from "snapback4/auth";
+import { api } from "../snapback/generated/api";
+const app = createApp({ url: "http://127.0.0.1:4400", sessionStore: localStorageSessionStore() });
+registerWebWitness(app, api);
+```
+
+`npm run build:web && snapback4 witness web dist --as alice` proves the first durable
+mutation reaches `sent`; `--mutation NAME --args JSON` supplies application
+values. Password accounts use `--email EMAIL --password-env ENV_VAR`, with
+`--url URL` for an existing backend. See `guide testing` for lifecycle and
+idempotence limits; this proves SDK integration, not the composer's UI.

 ## 4. The client
 
 **Constructor compatibility:** ready `Read<T>` values now require `existence`. Readers keep `isReady`/`isLoading`/`isDenied`; custom clients and mocks must classify their published value as `present`, `absent` or `partial`. No wire field was added. See `guide client` for the rules and point-read narrowing.

 ## Read boundaries
 `query(op, args)` settles ready/denied within 30 seconds including queues/approval. Admitted dependent writes: observed/failed or in complete predicted device answers. Unrelated writes pass; unknown intersects. Custody awaits events; loading/races before/after waits: four rounds; exhaustion refuses retryable `E_QUERY_RETRY`; proven permanently unavailable held reads refuse non-retryable `E_QUERY_HELD`. Offline: permitted predictions/data or immediate `E_OFFLINE`, including blockers. Refusals win; partial/stale permitted.
 
+## Password React and Expo
+
+Beside the persona form (`guide personas`), use this for `use identity password`:
+```tsx
+import { useState } from "react";
+import { createApp } from "snapback4/app";
+import { localStorageSessionStore } from "snapback4/auth";
+import { AppProvider, useAuth } from "snapback4/react";
+const app = createApp({ url: "http://127.0.0.1:4400", sessionStore: localStorageSessionStore() });
+export function PasswordApp() { return <AppProvider app={app}><Password /></AppProvider>; }
+function Password() {
+  const auth = useAuth(), [email, setEmail] = useState(""), [password, setPassword] = useState(""), [why, setWhy] = useState("");
+  if (auth.state === "restoring") return <>Restoring…</>;
+  if (auth.state === "signed-in") return <>Signed in as {auth.viewer}</>;
+  const submit = async (signup: boolean) => { const r = await (signup ? auth.signup(email, password) : auth.login(email, password)); if (!r.ok) setWhy(r.why.message); };
+  return <><input aria-label="Email" value={email} onChange={e => setEmail(e.target.value)} /><input aria-label="Password" type="password" value={password} onChange={e => setPassword(e.target.value)} />
+    <button onClick={() => void submit(false)}>Sign in</button><button onClick={() => void submit(true)}>Sign up</button>{why}</>;
+}
+```
+SDK: `{ok:true, session}` / `{ok:false, why}`; wire: `{session}` / `{denied}`. Expo imports `Platform` and `* as SecureStore`:
+`sessionStore: Platform.OS === "web" ? localStorageSessionStore() : { get: SecureStore.getItemAsync, set: SecureStore.setItemAsync, delete: SecureStore.deleteItemAsync }`.
+Synced web: `webDevice()`; native: `nativeDevice`; no-sync web: journal. `init --web`/`--expo` writes the form. `guide deploy` covers production.
+
 ## React hooks
 `matchRead(read, …)` (no type arguments; or a page) requires five states: `present(data, read)` excludes null; `partial(data, read)` keeps T; `denied(why, read)`; `absent(read)`/`loading(read)`. `otherwise(read)` covers omitted loading/absent/present; denied/partial stay explicit, or `...otherwiseAll(read => …)` anywhere takes all unnamed (named handlers win). `existence(read)` classifies; `isReady(read)` enables `read.existence`; `void client.close()` in cleanup.
 Types/hooks: `RowOf`/`ResultOf`/`ArgsOf`/`UseQuery`/`UsePage`/`useSignedInViewer()`/`usePersonaApp`; `{ skip: !id }` waits for arguments; `[add, remove].find(isFailed)?.last.why`; helpers take `Outcome | undefined` or `Read<unknown>` ([form](CLIENT-AND-OPERATIONS.md#status-components-and-a-form)).
@@ -64,6 +87,12 @@ Search: render `page.items`; `readPresentation(page.read)` supplies `pending` (l
 
 `app.online`/`useOnline()` (AppProvider): server query/observe, ephemeral mutate, act; no bearer/lifecycle; durable writes: app.mutate. `guide social`/`guide ephemeral`: snapshots/typing/quotes/bounded screens.
 
+## Generic read props and outcomes
+
+An empty complete list is `absent` only with `cut:null`, no continuation or blocking prediction. List props: `Read<T[]>` (`T extends { id: string }`); operation props: `UseQuery<typeof api.op>`.
+Narrow `write.state === "sent"` before `result` (omitted on replay); failed carries `why`; pending is not success.
+`useQuery` returns a live Read, without `refetch()`. For a one-shot refetch, `await client.query(...)` after `sent`.
+
 ## Row queries
 Share/paginate (`usePage` queries take `c: cursor ?` with `after c`); authorization remains dependent.
 Eight active; eight queued observation attempts start/turn; direct queries and timed presence refreshes use separate paths. Local budgets pause while queued, never replenish. Online evidence overtakes ≤8 times; occupied slots can withdraw.
@@ -81,33 +110,5 @@ Every milestone: `npm run export:ios`; proof in the background: `witness status
 `guide receipts`: `useSeen(api.receiptFamilies.chat, { scope, through, active })` owns retries. SDK owns delivery reporting; screen owns visible `seen` reporting. Supply visible confirmed positions; separate retained history/live decorations.
 
 ## One-view photos
-Mount **above the list**; key: `JSON.stringify([backend, account, messageId, assetId])`. Open once; recycling/refetch preserves handles. Unmount/account change revokes late responses. Close on `absent`/denied/tombstone/changed-asset/deadline; deadlines run through loading.
-
-```tsx
-import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
-import { useClient, useNow, useQuery } from "snapback4/react";
-import { isReady, existence } from "snapback4/contract";
-import { api } from "../snapback/generated/api";
-export function OneView({ messageId, assetId }: { messageId: string; assetId: string }) {
-  const client = useClient(), row = useQuery(api.messagePhoto, { id: messageId });
-  const handle = useRef<{ revoke(): void } | null>(null), alive = useRef(false), deadline = useRef<number | null>(null);
-  const [started, setStarted] = useState(false), [url, setUrl] = useState<string | null>(null), [problem, setProblem] = useState("");
-  const opened = useRef(false);
-  if (isReady(row) && row.existence === "present") deadline.current = row.data.expiresAt;
-  const now = useNow(deadline.current);
-  const closed = existence(row) === "denied" || existence(row) === "absent" || (isReady(row) && row.existence === "present" && (row.data.tombstone || row.data.photo?.id !== assetId)) || (deadline.current !== null && now >= deadline.current);
-  const invalid = useRef(false); invalid.current ||= closed;
-  useEffect(() => { alive.current = true; return () => { alive.current = false; handle.current?.revoke(); }; }, []);
-  useEffect(() => { if (closed) { handle.current?.revoke(); handle.current = null; setUrl(null); } }, [closed]);
-  async function open() {
-    if (opened.current || invalid.current || (!isReady(row) || row.existence !== "present" || row.revalidating)) return;
-    opened.current = true; setStarted(true);
-    const result = await client.asset(assetId);
-    if ("denied" in result) { if (alive.current) setProblem(result.denied.message); return; }
-    if (!alive.current || invalid.current) { result.revoke(); return; }
-    handle.current = result; setUrl(result.url);
-  }
-  return invalid.current ? null : <>{url ? <img src={url} alt="One-view photo" /> : <button disabled={started || !isReady(row) || row.existence !== "present" || row.revalidating} onClick={() => void open()}>Open photo</button>}{problem}</>;
-}
-```
+[One-view photo recipe](CLIENT-AND-OPERATIONS.md): mount above the list; retain handles across refetch; account/unmount revokes late responses; absence/denial/deadline closes.
 `useAsset` revokes on unmount; once media cannot refetch (`E_ASSET_VIEWED`).
diff --git a/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/tests/guide/wave9_dx.rs b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/tests/guide/wave9_dx.rs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000..9b6fbebe73
--- /dev/null
+++ b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/tests/guide/wave9_dx.rs
@@ -0,0 +1,18 @@
+use super::*;
+
+#[test]
+fn jobs_catch_up_sweep_includes_rows_before_the_latest_period_and_repeats() {
+    let source = quarry(card("jobs").unwrap()).into_iter().find(|block| block.contains("job releaseDue(")).unwrap();
+    let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source)]);
+    assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
+    let mut engine = Engine::new(snapback4_core::Store::memory().unwrap(), bound.schema, bound.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program)).unwrap();
+    engine.seed([100, 500, 2000].into_iter().enumerate().map(|(n, due)| ("embargoes".into(), Value::Row(BTreeMap::from([
+        ("id".into(), id(&format!("due{n}"))), ("dueAt".into(), Value::Int(due)), ("released".into(), Value::Bool(false)),
+    ]).into())))).unwrap();
+    for _ in 0..2 {
+        let run = engine.run_job("releaseDue", args(&[("periodStart", Value::Int(400)), ("periodEnd", Value::Int(1000))]), 1000).unwrap();
+        assert_eq!(run.state, JobState::Done, "{run:?}");
+        let rows = engine.store().rows("embargoes").unwrap();
+        for row in rows { assert_eq!(row.field("released").unwrap(), &Value::Bool(row.field("dueAt").unwrap().as_int().unwrap() < 1000)); }
+    }
+}

@@ -734,9 +734,25 @@ fn check_warns_about_installed_expo_core_version_mismatch() {
         let output = project.run(flags);
         assert!(output.status.success(), "{}", text(&output));
         let warning = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr);
-        assert!(warning.contains("snapback4 0.4.23") && warning.contains("snapback4-expo 0.4.22") && warning.contains("bun add snapback4@latest snapback4-expo@latest"), "{warning}");
+        assert!(warning.contains("snapback4 0.4.23") && warning.contains("snapback4-expo 0.4.22") && warning.contains("snapback4 upgrade"), "{warning}");
         if flags.contains(&"--json") { serde_json::from_slice::<serde_json::Value>(&output.stdout).unwrap(); }

     assert!(!text(&project.run(&["check"])).contains("WARNING"));
 }
+
+#[test]
+fn mutation_reply_omits_unrequested_id_and_keeps_result_id() {
+    let project = Project::new("use personas alice\ntable notes:\n  title: text 1..80\n  public\n  insert <- allow\nmutation add(title: text 1..80):\n  return insert notes { title }\nmutation refuse():\n  require false else NO_PROOF\n  return null\n");
+    let (_dev, url) = project.dev(0);
+    for path in ["/m/add", "/m/refuse"] {
+        let args = if path.ends_with("add") { r#"{"args":{"title":"first"}}"# } else { r#"{"args":{}}"# };
+        let (_, reply) = http(&url, "POST", path, Some("alice"), args);
+        assert!(!reply.as_object().unwrap().contains_key("id"), "{reply}");
+        if path.ends_with("add") { assert!(reply["result"]["id"].is_string(), "{reply}"); }
+    }
+    let (_, sent) = http(&url, "POST", "/m/add", Some("alice"), r#"{"id":"proof-write","args":{"title":"second"}}"#);
+    assert_eq!(sent["id"], "proof-write");
+    let (_, replay) = http(&url, "POST", "/m/add", Some("alice"), r#"{"id":"proof-write","args":{"title":"second"}}"#);
+    assert_eq!(replay["replayed"], true); assert!(replay.get("result").is_none());
+}
    let test = p.run(&["test", "--fixture", "fixture"]);
    assert!(!test.status.success());
    let text = format!("{}{}", String::from_utf8_lossy(&test.stdout), String::from_utf8_lossy(&test.stderr));
    assert!(text.contains("now complete — update the case"), "{text}");
    fs::write(p.0.join("fixture/test.cases.jsonl"), "{\"op\":\"history\",\"as\":\"alice\",\"args\":{\"parent\":\"p\"},\"expect\":\"complete\"}\n{\"op\":\"history\",\"as\":\"bob\",\"args\":{\"parent\":\"p\"}}\n").unwrap();
    let fixed = p.run(&["test", "--fixture", "fixture"]); assert!(fixed.status.success(), "{fixed:?}");
}

#[test]
fn production_writer_notes_cover_seed_only_and_bootstrap_cycles() {
    let p = Project::new();
    let tables = "use identity password\ntable staff:\n  principal: principal\n  unique principal\n  public\n  insert <- exists staff[viewer]\n  online only\nmutation addStaff(principal: principal):\n  return insert staff { principal }\ntable catalog:\n  title: text 1..80\n  public\n  online only\n";
    p.source(tables);
    let out = p.run(&["check", "--json"]);
    assert!(out.status.success(), "{out:?}");
    let json = parsed(&out);
    let notes: Vec<_> = json["notes"].as_array().unwrap().iter().filter(|n| n["code"] == "N_NO_PRODUCTION_WRITER").collect();
    assert_eq!(notes.len(), 2, "{json}");
    assert!(notes.iter().all(|n| n["rewrite"].as_str().unwrap().contains("first admin")));
    p.source(&tables.replace("insert <- exists staff[viewer]", "insert <- exists staff[viewer] or viewer = 'service:bootstrap'"));
    let out = p.run(&["check", "--json"]); assert!(out.status.success(), "{out:?}");
    let json = parsed(&out);
    assert_eq!(json["notes"].as_array().unwrap().iter().filter(|n| n["code"] == "N_NO_PRODUCTION_WRITER").count(), 1);
}

#[test]
fn fixture_arguments_are_checked_without_execution_and_saved_values_are_unknown() {
    let p = Project::new();
    p.source("use personas alice\nquery answer(n: int 1..5, text: text 2..8):\n  return n\n");
    fs::create_dir(p.0.join("fixtures")).unwrap();
    fs::write(p.0.join("fixtures/test.cases.jsonl"), "{\"op\":\"answer\",\"args\":{\"n\":6,\"text\":\"okay\"}}\n{\"op\":\"answer\",\"args\":{\"n\":2}}\n{\"op\":\"answer\",\"args\":{\"n\":\"$earlier\",\"text\":\"okay\"}}\n").unwrap();
    let out = p.run(&["check", "--json"]); assert!(out.status.success(), "{out:?}");
    let json = parsed(&out);
    let notes: Vec<_> = json["notes"].as_array().unwrap().iter().filter(|n| n["code"] == "N_FIXTURE_ARGUMENT_DRIFT").collect();
    assert_eq!(notes.len(), 2, "{json}");
    assert_eq!(notes[0]["site"]["line"], 1); assert_eq!(notes[1]["site"]["line"], 2);
    assert!(!p.store().exists(), "check must not create or mutate a fixture store");
}

#[test]
fn production_readiness_follows_positive_cycles_and_read_through_parents() {
    let p = Project::new();
    let notes = |source: &str| {
        p.source(source); let out = p.run(&["check", "--json"]); assert!(out.status.success(), "{out:?}");
        parsed(&out)["notes"].as_array().unwrap().iter().filter(|n| n["code"] == "N_NO_PRODUCTION_WRITER").map(|n| n["message"].as_str().unwrap().split(':').next().unwrap().to_owned()).collect::<Vec<_>>()
    };
    let cycle = "use identity password\ntable a:\n  principal: principal\n  unique principal\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- exists b[viewer]\n  online only\ntable b:\n  principal: principal\n  unique principal\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- exists a[viewer]\n  online only\nmutation addA(principal: principal):\n  return insert a { principal }\nmutation addB(principal: principal):\n  return insert b { principal }\n";
    assert_eq!(notes(cycle), ["a", "b"]);
    assert!(notes(&cycle.replace("insert <- exists a[viewer]", "insert <- exists a[viewer] or viewer = 'service:bootstrap'")).is_empty());
    let through = "use identity password\ntable parents:\n  owner: principal\n  immutable owner\n  read <- .owner = viewer\n  insert <- .owner = viewer\n  online only\ntable children:\n  parent: parents\n  immutable parent\n  read through .parent\n  insert <- .parent.owner = viewer\n  online only\nmutation addChild(parent: parents):\n  return insert children { parent }\n";
    assert_eq!(notes(through), ["children", "parents"]);
    assert!(notes(&format!("{through}\nmutation addParent():\n  return insert parents {{ owner: viewer }}\n")).is_empty());
}
    let stdout = String::from_utf8_lossy(&dir.init(&["--web", "--port", "20010"]).stdout).into_owned();
    assert!(stdout.contains("http://127.0.0.1:20010") && stdout.contains("http://localhost:20011") && !stdout.contains("sets the backend to N"), "{stdout}");
    let dir = Dir::new();
    let stdout = String::from_utf8_lossy(&dir.init(&["--expo"]).stdout).into_owned();
    assert!(stdout.contains("`init --expo --port N` sets the client's URL"), "{stdout}");
    let help = Command::new(BIN).args(["init", "--help"]).output().unwrap();
    let help = String::from_utf8_lossy(&help.stdout);
    assert!(help.contains("--port N") && help.contains("the page on N+1") && help.contains("one flag moves all of them"), "{help}");
}

#[test]
fn wave8_web_client_chooses_principal_journal_for_both_identity_modes_when_nothing_syncs() {
    for identity in ["use identity", "use personas alice, bob"] {
        for sync in [false, true] {
            let dir = Dir::new(); fs::create_dir_all(dir.0.join("snapback")).unwrap();
            fs::write(dir.0.join("snapback/schema.q"), format!("{identity}\ntable notes:\n  owner: principal\n  body: text <=100\n  immutable owner\n  read <- .owner = viewer\n  insert <- .owner = viewer\n  {}\n", if sync { "sync to .owner" } else { "online only" })).unwrap();
            let output = dir.run("--web"); assert!(output.status.success(), "{output:?}");
            let client = fs::read_to_string(dir.0.join("src/client.ts")).unwrap();
            assert_eq!(client.contains("device: webDevice()"), sync, "{client}");
            assert_eq!(client.contains("from \"snapback4/web\""), sync);
            assert!(!client.contains("This schema syncs todos"));
            assert_eq!(client.contains("sessionStore: localStorageSessionStore()"), identity == "use identity");
            assert_eq!(client.contains("createApp({ url, persona"), identity.contains("personas"));
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn password_starters_and_expo_web_journal_follow_schema() {
    for flag in ["--web", "--expo"] {
        for identity in ["use identity password", "use personas alice"] {
            let dir = Dir::new(); fs::create_dir(dir.0.join("snapback")).unwrap();
            fs::write(dir.0.join("snapback/schema.q"), format!("{identity}\ntable notes:\n  body: text 1..80\n  public\n  online only\nmutation add(body: text 1..80):\n  return insert notes {{ body }}\n")).unwrap();
            let out = dir.run(flag); assert!(out.status.success(), "{out:?}");
            let app = fs::read_to_string(dir.0.join(if flag == "--expo" { "App.tsx" } else { "src/App.tsx" })).unwrap();
            if identity.contains("password") { assert!(app.contains("auth.signup") && app.contains("auth.login") && !app.contains("auth.guest"), "{app}"); }
            let client = fs::read_to_string(dir.0.join("src/client.ts")).unwrap();
            assert!(!client.contains("webDevice"), "{client}");
            assert!(client.contains("get(\"api\")") && client.contains("registerWebWitness"), "{client}");
            if flag == "--expo" { assert!(client.contains("nativeDevice"), "{client}"); }
            let pkg: Value = serde_json::from_str(&fs::read_to_string(dir.0.join("package.json")).unwrap()).unwrap();
            assert!(pkg["scripts"]["build:web"].is_string());
            if flag == "--expo" { assert!(pkg["scripts"]["build:web"].as_str().unwrap().contains("--clear")); }
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn adapt_switches_guest_password_and_persona_and_keeps_password_readme() {
    for mode in ["--web", "--expo"] {
        let dir = Dir::new(); assert!(dir.run(mode).status.success());
        let path = dir.0.join("snapback/schema.q");
        let original = fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap();
        for (identity, expected) in [("use identity password", "the password sign-in"), ("use personas alice", "a persona picker"), ("use identity password", "the password sign-in"), ("use identity", "the guest sign-in")] {
            let body = original.lines().filter(|line| !line.starts_with("use identity") && !line.starts_with("use personas")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("\n");
            fs::write(&path, format!("{identity}\n{body}\n")).unwrap();
            let out = dir.init(&["--adapt"]); assert!(out.status.success(), "{out:?}");
            let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout); assert!(text.contains(expected), "{text}");
            let app = fs::read_to_string(dir.0.join(if mode == "--web" { "src/App.tsx" } else { "App.tsx" })).unwrap();
            if identity.contains("password") {
                assert!(app.contains("auth.signup") && !app.contains("auth.guest"));
                let readme = fs::read_to_string(dir.0.join("README.md")).unwrap();
                assert!(!readme.contains("Choose \"Continue as a"), "{readme}");
                assert!(readme.contains("Sign up or sign in"), "{readme}");
            }
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn untouched_previous_release_clients_adapt_to_password_but_edits_stay_owned() {
    for (mode, previous) in [
        ("--web", include_str!("../src/verbs/init/starter/previous/web-client.ts")),
        ("--expo", include_str!("../src/verbs/init/starter/previous/expo-client.ts")),
        ("--web", include_str!("../src/verbs/init/starter/previous/web-persona-client.ts")),
        ("--expo", include_str!("../src/verbs/init/starter/previous/expo-persona-client.ts")),
    ] {
        let dir = Dir::new(); let persona = previous.contains("PERSONAS");
        if persona {
            fs::create_dir(dir.0.join("snapback")).unwrap();
            fs::write(dir.0.join("snapback/schema.q"), "use personas alice\nquery hello():\n  return 42\n").unwrap();
        }
        assert!(dir.run(mode).status.success());
        let client = previous.replace("PERSONAS", "[\"alice\"]").replace("127.0.0.1:4400", "127.0.0.1:4517");
        fs::write(dir.0.join("src/client.ts"), &client).unwrap();
        let schema = dir.0.join("snapback/schema.q");
        let body = fs::read_to_string(&schema).unwrap().lines().filter(|l| !l.starts_with("use identity") && !l.starts_with("use personas")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("\n");
        fs::write(schema, format!("use identity password\n{body}\n")).unwrap();
        let out = dir.init(&["--adapt"]); assert!(out.status.success(), "{out:?}");
        let app_path = dir.0.join(if mode == "--web" { "src/App.tsx" } else { "App.tsx" });
        let app = fs::read_to_string(&app_path).unwrap(); assert!(app.contains("auth.signup") && !app.contains("auth.guest()"), "{app}");
        let adapted = fs::read_to_string(dir.0.join("src/client.ts")).unwrap();
        assert!(adapted.contains("127.0.0.1:4517") && adapted.contains("registerWebWitness"));
        fs::write(dir.0.join("src/client.ts"), format!("{client}\n// An application edit.\n")).unwrap();
        let before = fs::read_to_string(&app_path).unwrap();
        assert!(dir.init(&["--adapt"]).status.success());
        assert_eq!(fs::read_to_string(app_path).unwrap(), before);
        assert!(fs::read_to_string(dir.0.join("src/client.ts")).unwrap().ends_with("// An application edit.\n"));
    }
}
# Deploy: upgrade, real accounts and schema evolution

Deploy into the running serve process without restarting it. A release is
immutable source over one durable store; production admits bearer tokens.

```sh
bunx snapback4 check
bunx snapback4 test
bunx snapback4 deploy ./deployment
bunx snapback4 serve ./deployment --port 4400
```

See [SERVER.md — Release lifecycle](SERVER.md#release-lifecycle).

## Upgrade

`snapback4 upgrade [version]` resolves once and exactly pins core and installed
Expo adapters with npm/bun/pnpm. Rebuild native apps after adapter changes;
commit the lockfile, run check/typecheck/acceptance. `test` uses ./fixtures;
`--fixture dir` overrides it. Use the password and first-admin recipes below.

## Deploy and password identity

Add `use identity password` to your schema (the recipe below includes it).
Deploy from the app directory; keep the deployment directory on durable storage:

`serve --port N` accepts 0 for an allocated port or an inclusive range A-B, choosing its first free port. Invalid input refuses E_INPUT. Stdout's first line is the actual URL; stderr includes `SNAPBACK4_READY verb=serve … url=…`. Wait for readiness before requests. For a redeploy, run deploy on the same host while serve stays running, then verify a read and write with a real account. Its private owner socket serializes activation; failed validation keeps the current release. A dev or older unreachable owner requires stopping that owned process before retrying. See SERVER.md#release-lifecycle.

Layout: `releases/N/` holds each backend; `store.sqlite` holds rows, sessions,
receipts and queues; `assets/` holds uploads; `releases.jsonl` records activation.
`current` is a **text file containing a release number**, not a symlink. The
database's release metadata is authoritative; the file is a convenience copy.
Back up the whole directory with the server and external workers stopped;
include SQLite sidecars, assets and releases. See CLIENT-AND-OPERATIONS.md §6
for the tested stopped-backup/restore procedure and external-effect caveats.

```sh
U=http://127.0.0.1:4400
curl -s "$U/auth/signup" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  -d '{"email":"admin@example.test","password":"replace-with-a-private-password"}'
# {session:{principal,kind,token,expiresAt,scope}}
curl -s "$U/auth/login" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  -d '{"email":"admin@example.test","password":"replace-with-a-private-password"}'
```

For an executable headless account-seeding recipe, open `guide auth` or `guide headless`.
Use `signup`/`login` from `snapback4/auth` in the app; keep the returned
session in its session store and send `Authorization: Bearer <token>`.
Read the `session` or `denied` body (guide wire): wrong credentials and a
disabled identity method are 401 E_AUTH. A production persona is 401;
`/seed` and other development doors are 403. Signup creates an account,
not an admin. Sessions survive ordinary restarts and deployments of the same
store until expiry or revocation. A reset/replaced store requires sign-in again;
never deploy by deleting the store to make adoption pass.

## First admin: a service seat

Grant the signed-up account's principal through an operator seat. This recipe
permits ops and existing admins to insert grants, with no public first-user race:

```quarry
use identity password
table admins:
  principal: principal
  unique principal
  read <- .principal = viewer or viewer = 'service:ops'
  insert <- viewer = 'service:ops' or exists admins[principal = viewer]
mutation grantAdmin(principal: principal):
  return insert admins { principal }
table notes:
  owner: principal
  body: text <=200
  archived: bool default false
  status: enum('draft', 'live') default 'draft'
  read <- .owner = viewer
  insert <- .owner = viewer
```

```sh
TOKEN=$(bunx snapback4 token --service ops --release ./deployment)
curl -s "$U/m/grantAdmin" -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" \
  -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '{"args":{"principal":"user:SIGNED_UP_ID"}}'
unset TOKEN
```

Mint on the server host, against the same store. Token stdout is just the token;
prose is stderr. Protect the token as an operator credential. A repeated grant
hits the unique key; this insert-only recipe never takes upsert's update path.
Add revoke/update rules deliberately if your application needs them.

## Migrations and adoption

Optional columns, new tables/operations, non-unique indexes, wider text bounds
and additional enum values are additive. A new required column needs a
**literal default** for existing rows: `archived: bool default false` above
fills missing values without rewriting old rows. A new required `default now`
or `default viewer` needs an empty table.

Run `snapback4 check --store ./deployment/store.sqlite` before deployment.
Without a store, check only compiles. Tightening text, removing enum values,
making a column required, unique indexes or stronger constraints need every
existing row to fit. Adoption refuses E_ADOPT when they do not; repair data
through authorized mutations under the old schema, then retry. For example,
adding 'archived' to status is safe; removing it after rows use it refuses.
Removed storage becomes dormant; name reuse needs `--restore-dormant`.
`--allow-destructive` on deploy/rollback does not prune or bypass validation.
Prefer expand → migrate data → contract, keeping old clients compatible.

## Rollback, restore and prune

Run `snapback4 rollback ./deployment` while serve stays running. It selects
an earlier immutable release; `rollback ./deployment --to N` also selects a
newer release to roll forward. This adopts source into today's rows, sessions
and effects. Removed tables, columns and indexes become dormant, inaccessible
to programs and sync. Restoring retained names with fresh source requires
`deploy ./deployment --restore-dormant`; rollback selects restoration explicitly.
Narrowed enums, references and uniqueness still require valid existing data.
Only the active release's referential actions run; deleting a dormant child's
parent invalidates restoration instead of applying the retired cascade/restrict.
Devices re-acquire their partitions on a changed generation; queued writes are
rechecked under the active program. `--allow-feed-adoption` permits the explicit
feed adoption step. Inspect `snapback4 releases ./deployment` after an ambiguous
reply. `snapback4 prune ./deployment` previews retained storage;
`prune ./deployment --allow-destructive` permanently drops all dormant storage.
There is no automatic collector. See [SERVER.md — Release lifecycle](SERVER.md#release-lifecycle)
for validation repairs, dormant assets, crash recovery and activation fencing.

## Inspection, proxies and TLS

`snapback4 sql 'SELECT id, archived FROM notes' --release ./deployment --json`
returns positional rows under `columns`; `--json-objects` returns object rows
with direct boolean columns as true/false. Schema-qualified relations, recursive
or shadowing/forward-reference CTEs need positional --json. Inspection bypasses read rules and can
show expired stored rows; it is an operator tool, not an app query surface.
Bind loopback behind a TLS proxy. Forward Authorization and preserve response
bodies; allow long polls without buffering or early timeouts. Non-loopback
HTTP requires `--i-understand-cleartext` and a trusted private hop behind TLS.
Identity rate keys use the socket peer, not forwarded headers. Serve refuses
personas and dev doors even through a local proxy; see SERVER.md for capacity.

## Authoring and proof refusals

N_NO_PRODUCTION_WRITER means no production insert path can bootstrap this table. Add a service-seat insert mutation.

E_UPGRADE means the package-manager upgrade did not complete. Repair the package-manager or registry error and rerun upgrade.

use super::*;
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum RuleTerm {
    /// One authorized point read through a row-local typed reference.
    Reference { table: String, column: String, field: String, old: bool },
    /// Bounded live reads; a distinct variant makes old decoders refuse.
    ReferenceChain { column: String, hops: Vec<RuleHop>, old: bool },
    Field(String),
    OldField(String),
    NextField(String),
    Viewer,
    Literal(Value),
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct RuleHop { pub table: String, pub field: String }

pub const MAX_RULE_HOPS: usize = 3;

impl RuleTerm {
    pub fn reference_source(&self) -> Option<(&str, bool)> {
        match self {
            Self::Reference { column, old, .. } | Self::ReferenceChain { column, old, .. } => Some((column, *old)),
            _ => None,
        }
    }
    pub fn references(&self) -> impl Iterator<Item = (&str, &str)> {
        let (one, many) = match self {
            Self::Reference { table, field, .. } => (Some((table.as_str(), field.as_str())), &[][..]),
            Self::ReferenceChain { hops, .. } => (None, hops.as_slice()),
            _ => (None, &[][..]),
        };
        one.into_iter().chain(many.iter().map(|hop| (hop.table.as_str(), hop.field.as_str())))
    }
}

impl RuleExpr {
    pub fn has_reference(&self) -> bool {
        self.any_term(&|t| t.reference_source().is_some())
    }
    pub fn any_term(&self, term: &impl Fn(&RuleTerm) -> bool) -> bool {
        match self {
            Self::Eq(a,b) | Self::Ne(a,b) | Self::Compare(_,a,b) => term(a) || term(b),
            Self::Exists { key, equalities, .. } | Self::Created { key, equalities, .. } => key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,t)|t)).any(term),
            Self::And(rs) | Self::Or(rs) => rs.iter().any(|r| r.any_term(term)),
            Self::Not(r) => r.any_term(term),
            _ => false,
        }
    }
}

pub(super) fn validate(schema: &Schema, source: &Table, term: &RuleTerm, update: bool, path: &str, issues: &mut Vec<SchemaIssue>) {
    let Some((column, old)) = term.reference_source() else { return; };
    let depth = term.references().count();
    let mut valid = !path.ends_with(".read") && (!old || update) && (1..=MAX_RULE_HOPS).contains(&depth)
        && !matches!(term, RuleTerm::ReferenceChain { .. } if depth < 2);
    let (mut owner, mut column) = (source, column);
    for (table, field) in term.references() {
        valid &= schema.maintain_for(table).is_none()
            && matches!(owner.columns.get(column).map(ColumnType::required), Some(ColumnType::Ref { table: t }) if t == table)
            && !owner.maintained.contains_key(column) && !owner.written_by.get(column).is_some_and(|a| !a.audience);
        let Some(target) = schema.table(table) else { valid = false; break; };
        valid &= target.ephemeral.is_none() && target.columns.contains_key(field)
            && !target.maintained.contains_key(field) && !target.written_by.get(field).is_some_and(|a| !a.audience);
        owner = target; column = field;
    }
    if !valid { issues.push(SchemaIssue { path: path.into(), message: "E_QUARRY_RULE_HOP: write rules allow up to three ordinary live references with audience-readable fields; use authorized mutation reads for other paths".into() }); }
}

pub(super) fn validate_term(schema: &Schema, table: &Table, term: &RuleTerm, update: bool, path: &str, issues: &mut Vec<SchemaIssue>) {
    match term {
        RuleTerm::Field(field) => {
            if !table.columns.contains_key(field) {
                issues.push(SchemaIssue { path: path.to_owned(), message: format!("rule reads undeclared column {field}") });
            }
        }
        RuleTerm::OldField(field) | RuleTerm::NextField(field) => {
            if !update {
                issues.push(SchemaIssue { path: path.to_owned(), message: "old and next images exist only in update rules".into() });
            } else if !table.columns.contains_key(field) {
                issues.push(SchemaIssue { path: path.to_owned(), message: format!("rule reads undeclared column {field}") });
            }
        }
        RuleTerm::Reference { .. } | RuleTerm::ReferenceChain { .. } => validate(schema, table, term, update, path, issues),
        RuleTerm::Viewer | RuleTerm::Literal(_) => {}
    }
}
//! Expanded helpers can write from any expression block.
// @ref LLP 3000.022#14.3 — legacy eligibility examines the complete expanded write footprint.
use super::*;
pub(super) fn stmts_write_tables(stmts: &[Stmt], out: &mut Vec<(String, &'static str)>) {
    for stmt in stmts {
        match stmt {
            Stmt::Insert { table, .. } | Stmt::InsertIfAbsent { table, .. } => out.push((table.clone(), "insert")),
            Stmt::Update { table, .. } => out.push((table.clone(), "update")),
            Stmt::Upsert { table, .. } => out.push((table.clone(), "upsert")),
            Stmt::WriteTarget { target, upsert, .. } => out.push((target.table.clone(), if *upsert { "upsert" } else { "update" })),
            Stmt::Delete { table, .. } => out.push((table.clone(), "delete")),
            Stmt::DeleteTarget { target } => out.push((target.table.clone(), "delete")),
            _ => {}
        }
        match stmt {
            Stmt::Const { value, .. } | Stmt::Let { value, .. } | Stmt::Assign { value, .. } | Stmt::Push { value, .. } | Stmt::Return(value) => expr_write_tables(value, out),
            Stmt::Emit { payload: value, once, .. } => { expr_write_tables(value, out); if let Some(value) = once { expr_write_tables(value, out); } },
            Stmt::If { condition, then_body, else_body } => {
                expr_write_tables(condition, out);
                stmts_write_tables(then_body, out);
                stmts_write_tables(else_body, out);
            }
            Stmt::For { domain, body, .. } => {
                match domain {
                    LoopDomain::Page(scan) => scan_write_tables(scan, out),
                    LoopDomain::ExpressionRange { start, end_exclusive } => { expr_write_tables(start, out); expr_write_tables(end_exclusive, out); }
                    LoopDomain::DerivedArray { value, .. } => expr_write_tables(value, out),
                    LoopDomain::Recurse(recurse) => {
                        expr_write_tables(&recurse.seed, out);
                    }
                    LoopDomain::LiteralRange { .. } | LoopDomain::ArgList { .. } => {}
                }
                stmts_write_tables(body, out);
            }
            Stmt::Require { condition, .. } => expr_write_tables(condition, out),
            Stmt::Check { condition, current } => {
                expr_write_tables(condition, out);
                for key in &current.key { expr_write_tables(key, out); }
            }
            Stmt::InsertIfAbsent { row, .. } | Stmt::Insert { row, .. } => expr_write_tables(row, out),
            Stmt::Update { key, patch, .. } => {
                expr_write_tables(key, out);
                expr_write_tables(patch, out);
            }
            Stmt::Upsert { key, row, .. } => {
                expr_write_tables(key, out);
                expr_write_tables(row, out);
            }
            Stmt::WriteTarget { target, patch, condition, .. } => {
                for key in &target.key { expr_write_tables(key, out); }
                expr_write_tables(patch, out);
                if let Some(condition) = condition { stmts_write_tables(&condition.setup, out); expr_write_tables(&condition.value, out); }
            }
            Stmt::Delete { key, .. } => expr_write_tables(key, out),
            Stmt::DeleteTarget { target } => {
                for key in &target.key { expr_write_tables(key, out); }
            }
            Stmt::Break | Stmt::Continue => {}
        }
    }
}

    if let Some(range) = &scan.range {

                    Ok(None) => {}

pub fn check(options: &Options) -> i32 {
    if let Some(file) = options.flags.get("emit-lean") {
        return emit_lean(file);
    }
    let project = project_or_exit();
    let started = Instant::now();
    package_versions::warn(&project);
    let mut bound = match fix::checked(if options.flags.contains_key("fix") { fix::compile(&project) } else { project.compile() }) {
        Ok(bound) => bound,
        Err(code) => return code,
    };
    if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty() {
        report_diagnostics(&bound, options.json, true, options.flags.contains_key("verbose"));
        if !options.json { print_prediction(&bound, options.flags.contains_key("verbose")); }
        return 1;
    }
    // @ref LLP 3000.007#18.3 — check judges adoption against the dev store's active generation and rows, read-only
    match adoption::diagnostics(&project, &bound.schema, options.flags.get("store")) {
        Ok((problems, notes)) => { bound.diagnostics.extend(problems); bound.notes.extend(notes); },
        Err(error) => { eprintln!("cannot check store adoption: {error}"); return 2; }
    }
    if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty() {
        report_diagnostics(&bound, options.json, true, options.flags.contains_key("verbose"));
        if !options.json { print_prediction(&bound, options.flags.contains_key("verbose")); }
        return 1;
    }
    bound.notes.extend(readiness::notes(&project, &bound, options.flags.get("fixture")));
    bound.notes.extend(snapback4_lang::bind::residency::change_notes(&bound, &residency::previous(&project)));
    if let Err(error) = project.write_generated(&bound) {
        eprintln!("cannot write generated types: {error}");
        return 2;
    }
    if let Err(error) = project.check_server() {
        if options.json { println!("{}", json!({ "ok": false, "problems": [{ "code": if error.starts_with("E_SERVER_TOOLCHAIN") { "E_SERVER_TOOLCHAIN" } else { "E_SERVER_TYPECHECK" }, "message": error, "guide": "actions" }] })); }
        else { eprintln!("{error}"); }
            "replay the same id first", "only a recorded failure",
            "retry a recorded failure with a new id after cleanup",
            "fresh-id recovery", "bare denied stays uncertain",
        ] {
            assert!(!lower.contains(obsolete), "{name}: obsolete {obsolete}");
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn delivery_bound_rfc_acceptance_requires_start_only_recovery() {
    let path = std::path::Path::new(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))
        .join("../../../llp/3000.025-expiring-mutation-read-prefix.rfc.md");
    let rfc = std::fs::read_to_string(path).unwrap();
    for (number, required) in [
        (5, vec!["onDeliveryRefused once", "next start sends a new id", "delivery-refusal.test.mjs"]),
        (6, vec!["E_BOUND is an ordinary non-retryable refusal", "calls onDeliveryRefused once",
            "until the next start", "with a new id"]),
        (7, vec!["start-only queue witnesses prove §3's policy", "one onDeliveryRefused",
            "a new id only at the next start"]),
    ] {
        let section = rfc.split_once(&format!("\n## {number}. ")).unwrap().1
            .split("\n## ").next().unwrap();
        let text = section.replace(['`', '*'], "").split_whitespace().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" ");
        for phrase in required.into_iter().chain([
            "no retry within the session", "committed report that runs again is a no-op report",
        ]) {
            assert!(text.contains(phrase), "LLP 3000.025 §{number}: missing {phrase}");
        }
        let lower = text.to_lowercase();
        for obsolete in ["fresh id without restart", "replay the same id first", "replay-confirmed"] {
            assert!(!lower.contains(obsolete), "LLP 3000.025 §{number}: obsolete {obsolete}");
        }
    }
}

/// synced when it was written.
pub(super) fn without_offline(text: &str) -> String {
    let legacy = [
        ("the Snapback 4 web starter. It works offline and syncs when the link returns.\n", "the Snapback 4 web starter.\n\nOFFLINE\n"),
        ("started from the Snapback 4 Expo starter. Development and production builds\nopen the native device and work offline; Expo Go uses the online client; web\nuses the wasm device.\n",
            "started from the Snapback 4 Expo starter. Development and production builds\nopen the native device; Expo Go uses the online client; web uses the wasm\ndevice.\n\nOFFLINE\n"),
    ];
    let mut text = text.to_owned();
    for (old, new) in legacy { text = text.replacen(old, new, 1); }
    text.split("\n\n").map(|paragraph| if paragraph.starts_with("Offline") { "OFFLINE" } else { paragraph }).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("\n\n")
}

/// Which starter screen pair: the guest sign-in, or the persona picker.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Debug)]
pub(super) enum Platform { Web, Expo }

impl Platform {
    /// Where the starter writes its screen and its client.
    pub(super) fn paths(self) -> (&'static str, &'static str) {
        match self { Platform::Web => ("src/App.tsx", "src/client.ts"), Platform::Expo => ("App.tsx", "src/client.ts") }
    }
}

/// The screen a signed-in viewer sees: the starter's `Todos`, which calls
/// list/add/toggle/remove, or `Home`, which compiles against any schema (the
/// viewer and the generated api's operation names, for the project to replace),
/// with one real read when the schema has a query that takes no required
/// arguments (its `api` path, `notes` or `module.notes`).
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#7.4 — a starter screen must typecheck against the schema beside it.
// @ref LLP 3000.013#6.6 — wave 5: the adapted Home renders one useQuery through matchRead, the idioms Todos taught.
#[derive(Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Debug)]
pub(super) enum Body { Todos, Home(Option<String>) }

/// The starter's screen and client for a schema: the guest sign-in, or, with
/// `personas`, the persona picker, around `body`. The texts are as the
/// starter ships them, before init's port rewrite.
pub(super) fn screens(platform: Platform, personas: Option<&[String]>, body: &Body) -> (String, String) {
    let (guest, guest_client, persona, persona_client, todos, home) = match platform {
        Platform::Web => (include_str!("starter/web/guest.tsx"), include_str!("starter/web/client.ts"), include_str!("starter/personas/web-app.tsx"), include_str!("starter/personas/web-client.ts"), include_str!("starter/web/todos.tsx"), include_str!("starter/web/home.tsx")),
        Platform::Expo => (include_str!("starter/expo/guest.tsx"), include_str!("starter/expo/client.ts"), include_str!("starter/personas/expo-app.tsx"), include_str!("starter/personas/expo-client.ts"), include_str!("starter/expo/todos.tsx"), include_str!("starter/expo/home.tsx")),
    };
    let first_read = match platform { Platform::Web => include_str!("starter/web/first-read.tsx"), Platform::Expo => include_str!("starter/expo/first-read.tsx") };
    let (head, client) = match personas {
        None => (guest, guest_client.to_owned()),
        Some(personas) => {
            let list = personas.iter().map(|name| serde_json::to_string(name).unwrap()).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
            (persona, persona_client.replace("PERSONAS", &format!("[{list}]")))
        }
    };
    let app = match body {
        Body::Todos => compose(head, todos),
        Body::Home(op) => {
            let (head, home) = if personas.is_some() { (persona_head_home(head), persona_home(home)) } else { (head.replace("<Todos />", "<Home />"), home.to_owned()) };
            match op {
                None => compose(&head, &home.replace("    <FirstRead />\n", "")),
                Some(op) => compose(&compose(&head, &home), &first_read.replace("api.OP", &format!("api.{op}"))),
            }
        }
    };
    (super::without_design_references(&app), super::without_design_references(&client))
}

        client.replace("import { webDevice } from \"snapback4/web\";\n", "")

fn evaluate_leaf(rule: &RuleExpr, viewer: &str, old: Option<&Value>, next: &Value, created_allowed: bool, probe: &mut dyn Probe, missing: &mut bool) -> Result<bool, crate::error::RuntimeError> {
    match rule {
        RuleExpr::Public { .. } | RuleExpr::Allow => Ok(true),
        RuleExpr::Deny => Ok(false),
        RuleExpr::Eq(left, right) => Ok(term(left, viewer, old, next, probe, missing)? == term(right, viewer, old, next, probe, missing)?),
        RuleExpr::Ne(left, right) => Ok(term(left, viewer, old, next, probe, missing)? != term(right, viewer, old, next, probe, missing)?),
        RuleExpr::Compare(op,left,right) => {
            let left = term(left, viewer, old, next, probe, missing)?;
            let right = term(right, viewer, old, next, probe, missing)?;
            let (Value::Int(a),Value::Int(b)) = (left,right) else { return Ok(false); };
            Ok(match op { crate::ir::BinaryOp::Gte => a >= b, crate::ir::BinaryOp::Gt => a > b,
                crate::ir::BinaryOp::Lte => a <= b, crate::ir::BinaryOp::Lt => a < b, _ => false })
        }
        RuleExpr::And(_) | RuleExpr::Or(_) | RuleExpr::Not(_) => unreachable!("compound rule"),
        RuleExpr::Exists { table, index, key, equalities, .. } | RuleExpr::Created { table, index, key, equalities, .. } => {
            // Bind each operand once, through one codec/allocation path for both leaves.
            let created = matches!(rule, RuleExpr::Created { .. });
            if created && !created_allowed {
                return Err(crate::error::RuntimeError::Schema("created probes are valid only in insert rules".into()));
            }
            let key = key.iter().map(|term| self::term(term, viewer, old, next, probe, missing)).collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()?;
            if *missing { return Ok(false); }
            if created {
                if !probe.created(table, index, &key) { return Ok(false); }
                if equalities.is_empty() { return Ok(true); }
            }
            let equalities = equalities.iter().map(|(column, value)| Ok((column.clone(), term(value, viewer, old, next, probe, missing)?))).collect::<Result<Vec<_>, crate::error::RuntimeError>>()?;
            if *missing { return Ok(false); }
            probe.exists(table, index, &key, &equalities)
        }
    }
}

pub(super) fn term(term: &RuleTerm, viewer: &str, old: Option<&Value>, next: &Value, probe: &mut dyn Probe, missing: &mut bool) -> Result<Value, crate::error::RuntimeError> {
    Ok(match term {
        RuleTerm::Field(field) | RuleTerm::NextField(field) => { probe.field(field, false); next.field(field).cloned().unwrap_or(Value::Null) },
        RuleTerm::OldField(field) => { probe.field(field, true); old.and_then(|old| old.field(field).ok()).cloned().unwrap_or(Value::Null) },
        RuleTerm::Reference { column, old: previous, .. } | RuleTerm::ReferenceChain { column, old: previous, .. } => {
            probe.field(column, *previous);
            let row = if *previous { old } else { Some(next) };
            let mut value = row.and_then(|r| r.field(column).ok()).cloned().unwrap_or(Value::Null);
            for (table, field) in term.references() {
                match probe.reference(table, &value, field)? {

#[test]
fn password_starters_and_expo_web_journal_follow_schema() {
    for flag in ["--web", "--expo"] {
        for identity in ["use identity password", "use personas alice"] {
            let dir = Dir::new(); fs::create_dir(dir.0.join("snapback")).unwrap();
            fs::write(dir.0.join("snapback/schema.q"), format!("{identity}\ntable notes:\n  body: text 1..80\n  public\n  online only\nmutation add(body: text 1..80):\n  return insert notes {{ body }}\n")).unwrap();
            let out = dir.run(flag); assert!(out.status.success(), "{out:?}");
            let app = fs::read_to_string(dir.0.join(if flag == "--expo" { "App.tsx" } else { "src/App.tsx" })).unwrap();
            if identity.contains("password") { assert!(app.contains("auth.signup") && app.contains("auth.login") && !app.contains("auth.guest"), "{app}"); }
            let client = fs::read_to_string(dir.0.join("src/client.ts")).unwrap();
            assert!(!client.contains("webDevice"), "{client}");
            assert!(client.contains("get(\"api\")") && client.contains("registerWebWitness"), "{client}");
            if flag == "--expo" { assert!(client.contains("nativeDevice"), "{client}"); }
            let pkg: Value = serde_json::from_str(&fs::read_to_string(dir.0.join("package.json")).unwrap()).unwrap();
            assert!(pkg["scripts"]["build:web"].is_string());
            if flag == "--expo" { assert!(pkg["scripts"]["build:web"].as_str().unwrap().contains("--clear")); }
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn adapt_switches_guest_password_and_persona_and_keeps_password_readme() {
    for mode in ["--web", "--expo"] {
        let dir = Dir::new(); assert!(dir.run(mode).status.success());
        let path = dir.0.join("snapback/schema.q");
        let original = fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap();
        for (identity, expected) in [("use identity password", "the password sign-in"), ("use personas alice", "a persona picker"), ("use identity password", "the password sign-in"), ("use identity", "the guest sign-in")] {
            let body = original.lines().filter(|line| !line.starts_with("use identity") && !line.starts_with("use personas")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("\n");
            fs::write(&path, format!("{identity}\n{body}\n")).unwrap();
            let out = dir.init(&["--adapt"]); assert!(out.status.success(), "{out:?}");
            let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout); assert!(text.contains(expected), "{text}");
            let app = fs::read_to_string(dir.0.join(if mode == "--web" { "src/App.tsx" } else { "App.tsx" })).unwrap();
            if identity.contains("password") {
                assert!(app.contains("auth.signup") && !app.contains("auth.guest"));
                let readme = fs::read_to_string(dir.0.join("README.md")).unwrap();
                assert!(!readme.contains("Choose \"Continue as a"), "{readme}");
                assert!(readme.contains("Sign up or sign in"), "{readme}");
            }
        }
    }
}

pub(super) fn term(term: &RuleTerm, viewer: &str, old: Option<&Value>, next: &Value, probe: &mut dyn Probe, missing: &mut bool) -> Result<Value, crate::error::RuntimeError> {
    Ok(match term {
//! The npm wrapper (`bin/snapback4.js`) runs this binary as its child and
//! forwards SIGINT, SIGTERM and SIGHUP. A SIGKILL to the wrapper cannot be
//! forwarded, so the wrapper names itself in `SNAPBACK4_SUPERVISOR_PID` and the
//! binary stops itself, with SIGTERM's orderly cleanup, once that parent is
//! gone: on Linux the kernel delivers it (`PR_SET_PDEATHSIG`), elsewhere a
//! thread notices the reparenting within 250 ms.
// @ref LLP 3000.011.004.000#6 — a scripted dev stops with its wrapper, even one killed by SIGKILL.
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicU32, Ordering};

pub(crate) const ENV: &str = "SNAPBACK4_SUPERVISOR_PID";
static SUPERVISOR: AtomicU32 = AtomicU32::new(0);

/// The wrapper's PID when this process runs under it.
pub(crate) fn pid() -> Option<u32> { Some(SUPERVISOR.load(Ordering::Acquire)).filter(|pid| *pid != 0) }

/// Process entry, before any thread starts: read and clear the wrapper's PID
/// (children such as the dev worker must not inherit it), then tie this
/// process's life to it.
pub(crate) fn adopt() {
    let Some(value) = std::env::var_os(ENV) else { return };
    // SAFETY: process entry, before any thread is started.
    unsafe { std::env::remove_var(ENV) };
    let Some(pid) = value.to_str().and_then(|text| text.parse::<u32>().ok()).filter(|pid| *pid > 1) else { return };
    // A variable inherited from elsewhere names someone else's process: only
    // the parent that launched us supervises us.
    let parent = unsafe { libc::getppid() } as u32;
    if parent != pid {
        let alive = unsafe { libc::kill(pid as libc::pid_t, 0) } == 0 || std::io::Error::last_os_error().raw_os_error() == Some(libc::EPERM);
        if alive { return; }
        // The wrapper died between spawning us and now: its server must not start.
        eprintln!("SNAPBACK4_SIGNAL reason=\"supervisor {pid} exited before start\"");
        std::process::exit(128 + libc::SIGTERM);
    }
    SUPERVISOR.store(pid, Ordering::Release);
    #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
    unsafe {
        libc::prctl(libc::PR_SET_PDEATHSIG, libc::SIGTERM as libc::c_ulong, 0, 0, 0);
        // The parent may have died before the request took effect.
        if libc::getppid() as u32 != pid { libc::kill(libc::getpid(), libc::SIGTERM); }
    }
    #[cfg(not(target_os = "linux"))]
    {
        let _ = std::thread::Builder::new().name("supervisor".into()).spawn(move || loop {
            std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(250));
            if unsafe { libc::getppid() } as u32 != pid {
                unsafe { libc::kill(libc::getpid(), libc::SIGTERM); }
                return;
            }
        });
    }
}
#!/usr/bin/env node
// `npx snapback4 <verb>`: the Rust binary, found in the platform package
// installed beside this one, or named by SNAPBACK4_BIN, or built in this
// repository. The JavaScript here only finds and runs it, and the two live and
// die together: SIGINT, SIGTERM and SIGHUP to this wrapper are forwarded, and
// SIGKILL, which cannot be forwarded, still stops the binary: it is told this
// wrapper's PID (SNAPBACK4_SUPERVISOR_PID) and exits when its parent is gone
// (Linux: the kernel's parent-death signal; elsewhere within 250 ms). So a
// recorded PID of either stops the server; `GET /dev/status` reports both
// (`pid` is the binary, `wrapperPid` this wrapper), and so does the stderr line
// `SNAPBACK4_READY … url=… pid=… wrapperPid=…`.
import { spawn } from "node:child_process";
import { resolveBinary } from "../dist/resolve-binary.js";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import { constants } from "node:os";

if (process.argv[2] === "web-shell") {
  try { await (await import("./web-shell.js")).runWebShell(process.argv.slice(3)); }
  catch (error) { console.error(`E_INPUT at web-shell: ${error.message}\n  rewrite: export a static app, then run snapback4 web-shell dist --base /`); process.exit(2); }
  process.exit(0);
}

let binary;
try { binary = resolveBinary(); }
catch (error) { console.error(error.message); process.exit(2); }
// Internal export helpers share the wrapper's kernel lease through fd 3.
const exportLease = process.argv[2] === "witness" && ["export-lease", "export-publish"].includes(process.argv[3]);
const child = spawn(binary, process.argv.slice(2), { stdio: exportLease ? ["inherit", "inherit", "inherit", 3] : "inherit", env: {
  ...process.env, SNAPBACK4_ACQUIRE_HELPER: fileURLToPath(new URL("./witness-acquire.js", import.meta.url)),
  SNAPBACK4_UPGRADE_HELPER: fileURLToPath(new URL("./upgrade.js", import.meta.url)),
  SNAPBACK4_WEB_HELPER: fileURLToPath(new URL("./witness-web.js", import.meta.url)),
  SNAPBACK4_SUPERVISOR_PID: String(process.pid),
} });
const handlers = new Map(["SIGINT", "SIGTERM", "SIGHUP"].map(signal => [signal, () => { try { child.kill(signal); } catch { /* gone */ } }]));
for (const [signal, handler] of handlers) process.on(signal, handler);
// `npx snapback4 dev` puts npm and `sh -c` above this wrapper. A SIGTERM to the
// npx PID kills npm and the shell but never reaches us, so the server would keep
// serving as an orphan. Our parent's death is the stop signal. (`process.ppid` is
// read once at startup, so probe the original parent instead.)
const parent = process.ppid;
const parentAlive = () => { try { process.kill(parent, 0); return true; } catch (error) { return error.code === "EPERM"; } };
const orphanWatch = parent > 1 ? setInterval(() => {
  if (!parentAlive()) { try { child.kill("SIGTERM"); } catch { /* gone */ } }
}, 250) : null;
orphanWatch?.unref();
child.on("exit", (code, signal) => {
  if (orphanWatch) clearInterval(orphanWatch);
  for (const [name, handler] of handlers) process.off(name, handler);
  if (signal) {
    // Restore the default disposition before re-raising. Some platforms do not
    // terminate on this call; never let their empty event loop report success.
    const status = 128 + (constants.signals[signal] ?? { SIGHUP: 1, SIGINT: 2, SIGTERM: 15 }[signal] ?? 1);
    setTimeout(() => process.exit(status), 100);
    try { process.kill(process.pid, signal); } catch { process.exit(status); }
    return;
  }
  process.exit(code ?? 1);
});
